    Easou Technology Holdings Limited is an exempted company                  宜搜科技控股有限公司為一家於2022年2月9日在
    incorporated in the Cayman Islands with limited liability on February     開曼群島註冊成立的獲豁免有限公司，並於2024
    9, 2022, and was listed on the Main Board of the Stock Exchange on        年6月7日在聯交所主板上市。
    June 7, 2024.

    Since its establishment in 2005, the Group has been deeply involved       本 集 團 自 2005 年 成 立 以 來 ， 深 耕 推 薦 技 術 的 研
    in the R&D of recommendation technology and continuously                  發，持續優化自有的智能推薦引擎－宜搜推薦引
    optimized its proprietary intelligent recommendation engine, Easou        擎。本集團主要從事AI應用板塊，包括數字閱讀平
    Recommendation Engine. The Group is primarily engaged in the AI           台服務、數字營銷服務、網絡遊戲發行服務及其他
    application sector, which includes online reading platform services,      數字內容服務。此外，本集團正在探索RWA及其
    digital marketing services, online games publishing services and          他數字資產板塊。
    other digital content services. In addition, the Group is exploring the
    RWA and other digital assets sector.

    Leveraging its independently-developed AI-based recommendation            本集團以自主研發的AI推薦技術為核心競爭力，持
    technology as its core competitive advantage, the Group continues         續升級包含六層架構的宜搜推薦引擎。目前該技術
    to upgrade its six-layered Easou Recommendation Engine. This              已成功應用於數字閱讀、數字營銷、網絡遊戲等業
    technology has currently been successfully applied to online reading,     務場景，並正積極拓展至更多領域。尤其在RWA
    digital marketing, online games and other business scenarios, and is      領域，本集團正積極探索AI推薦技術與區塊鏈技術
    being actively expanded to more fields. In particular, within the RWA     的融合應用，打造數字資產的價值發現平台。
    sector, the Group is actively exploring the integrated application of

    create a value discovery platform for digital assets.

    content, online games and music, among others. The platform               道、用戶、廣告客戶及電信運營商等多方參與者，

MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
管理層討論及分析



Business Review                                                           業務回顧

Since its establishment in 2005, the Group has been deeply involved       本 集 團 自 2005 年 成 立 以 來 ， 深 耕 推 薦 技 術 的 研
in the R&D of recommendation technology and continuously                  發，持續優化自有的智能推薦引擎－宜搜推薦引
optimized its proprietary intelligent recommendation engine, Easou        擎。本集團主要從事AI應用板塊，包括數字閱讀平
Recommendation Engine. The Group is primarily engaged in the AI           台服務、數字營銷服務、網絡遊戲發行服務和其他
application sector, which includes online reading platform services,      數字內容服務。此外，本集團正在探索RWA及其
digital marketing services, online games publishing services and          他數字資產板塊。
other digital content services. In addition, the Group is exploring the
RWA and other digital assets sector.

In 2025, the Group further invested in the R&D of AI generated            2025年，本集團進一步投入在AI生成內容(AIGC)與
content (AIGC) and intelligent recommendation algorithms, with            智能推薦算法方面的研發，全年研發支出達人民幣
annual R&D expenditures reaching RMB50.3 million, providing               50.3百萬元，有力支撐了AI應用業務板塊的提升。

          adversely affected.

provided that the expected demand for the use of funds is ensured,      以確保，本公司可(i)將6月認購事項及7月認購事項
the Company may (i) hold the unused proceeds from the June              的未動用所得款項存放於持牌商業銀行及╱或認可
Subscription and July Subscription in short-term interest-bearing       金融機構之短期計息賬戶；或(ii)認購安全性高、
accounts at licensed commercial banks and/or authorized financial       流動性佳且期限不超過十二個月的理財產品，以提
institutions; or (ii) subscribe for wealth management products with     升本集團的資金使用效率及回報，惟該等理財產品
high security and good liquidity and a period not exceeding twelve      僅可於經董事會批准並符合相關上市規則後認購
months, so as to improve the utilization efficiency of the Group’s      （「動用閒置資金」）。
funds and its return, provided that the subscription of such wealth
management products should only be made with the approval of
the Board and in compliance with the relevant Listing Rules (the
“Utilization of Idle Funds”).

The Company believes that the Utilization of Idle Funds will enhance    本公司認為，動用閒置資金將有助於提升本集團財
the Group’s allocation of financial resources and increase the          務資源的分配，提高閒置資金的使用效率，這將進
utilization efficiency of idle funds, which will further increase the   一步增加本集團整體盈利能力，並提高本公司的投
overall profitability of the Group and improve the investment returns   資回報。此外，該等活動不會影響本集團日常營運
for the Company. Further, such activities shall not affect the daily    資金需求或本集團主要業務營運。
working capital needs of the Group or the Group’s operation of the
principal business.

     practicable; and (ii) periodic on-the-job training, such as technology    在提升中高層管理人員的創新、領導及溝通能力。
     training, which aims to enhance our employees’ professional
     skills, knowledge and understanding of the latest technological
     developments in the industry, and management training, which aims
     to improve the abilities in innovation, leadership and communication
     of our mid-level and senior managers.

We adhere to the principles of fairness, justice and reasonable            我們秉持公平、公正、合理的薪酬原則，為僱員提
remuneration and provide our employees with competitive                    供具競爭力的薪酬及福利。僱員的薪酬待遇主要包
remuneration and benefits. The remuneration package of employees           括基本薪金、表現相關獎金、養老金計劃供款及社
mainly includes basic salary, performance-related compensation,            會福利獎金。僱員的績效目標主要基於其職位及部
pension scheme, contributions and social welfare bonus. The                門而釐定，同時定期審查彼等的績效，並根據該等
performance targets of employees are primarily determined                  審查的結果釐定其薪金、獎金及晉升評估。
according to their positions and departments, and regular
performance review will be conducted, and salaries, bonus and
promotion appraisals will be determined based on appraisal results.


RETIREMENT AND EMPLOYEE BENEFITS SCHEMES                                   退休及僱員福利計劃

The employees of the Group’s subsidiaries which operate in China           本集團於中國營運的附屬公司的僱員須參加當地市
are required to participate in a central pension scheme operated by        政府實施的中央養老金計劃。該等附屬公司須就僱
the local municipal government. These subsidiaries are required to         員薪金按若干百分比向中央養老金計劃供款。該等
contribute a certain proportion of their payroll costs to the central      供款於根據中央養老金計劃的規則成為應付款項時
pension scheme. The contributions are charged to profit or loss as         自損益扣除。
they become payable in accordance with the rules of the central
pension scheme.

Details of the retirement and employee benefits schemes of the             有關本公司退休及僱員福利計劃的詳情載於綜合財
Company are set out in note 2.4 to the consolidated financial              務報表附註2.4。
statements.

      Listing Rules Implications                                                上市規則涵義

      The transactions contemplated under the Contractual Arrangements          合約安排項下擬進行的交易為本公司的持續關連交
      are continuing connected transactions for our Company and are             易，因此須遵守上市規則第14A章項下申報、公告
      subject to reporting, announcement and independent Shareholders’          及獨立股東批准之規定。董事（包括獨立非執行董
      approval requirements under Chapter 14A of the Listing Rules. Our         事）認為(i)合約安排對本集團的法律架構及業務經
      Directors, including our independent non-executive Directors, are         營至為重要；及(ii)合約安排乃按一般商業條款或
      of the view that (i) the Contractual Arrangements are fundamental         有利於本集團的條款，在本集團的日常業務過程中
      to our Group’s legal structure and business operations; and (ii) the      訂立，屬公平合理或對本集團有利，並符合股東的
      Contractual Arrangements are on normal commercial terms or                整體利益。因此，儘管合約安排項下擬進行的交
      on terms more favorable to our Group, in the ordinary and usual           易在技術上構成上市規則第14A章下的持續關連交
      course of our Group’s business and are fair and reasonable or to the      易，但董事認為，鑒於本集團在合約安排項下關連
      advantage of our Group and are in the interests of our Shareholders       交易規則方面處於特殊情況，就合約安排項下擬進
      as a whole. Accordingly, notwithstanding that the transactions            行的所有交易而言，若嚴格遵守上市規則第14A章
      contemplated under the Contractual Arrangements technically               所載規定，包括（其中包括）公告及獨立股東批准之
      constitute continuing connected transactions under Chapter 14A            規定，將會令本公司承擔過重的負擔且不切實際，
      of the Listing Rules, our Directors consider that, given that our         並會增加本公司不必要的行政成本。
      Group is placed in a special situation in relation to the connected
      transactions rules under the Contractual Arrangements, it would be
      unduly burdensome and impracticable, and would add unnecessary
      administrative costs to our Company, for all the transactions
      contemplated under the Contractual Arrangements to be subject to
      strict compliance with the requirements set out under Chapter 14A of
      the Listing Rules, including, among other things, the announcement
      and approval of independent Shareholders.

      the Nomination Committee will hold a meeting and/or by                 委員會將舉行會議及╱或以書面決議案方式

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                     風險管理及內部控制

The Board acknowledges that (i) it is the responsibility of the Board    董事會確認，(i)董事會有責任維持足夠的內部控制
for maintaining an adequate internal control system to safeguard         系統以保障股東投資及本公司資產，並每年檢討系
the Shareholders’ investments and the Company’s assets and               統之成效；及(ii)本公司的風險管理及內部控制系
reviewing the effectiveness of such system on an annual basis and;       統適當及有效達到企業管治守則的原則D2所述目
(ii) the Company risk management and internal control systems            的。本集團風險管理及內部控制系統有明確權限的
are appropriate and effective for the purposes set out in Principle      管理架構及完善的政策及程序，旨在促進業務合
D2 of the CG Code. The Group’s risk management and internal              法、合規及有效運營，確保財務報告的可靠性及遵
control systems are featured with a defined management structure         守適用的法律法規、識別及管理潛在風險及保障本
with limits of authority and well- rounded policies and procedures,      集團資產的安全。風險管理及內部控制系統旨在管
and are designed to facilitate legal, compliant and effective            理而非消除未能實現業務目標的風險，且僅可合理
operation of business, to ensure reliability of financial reporting      而非絕對保證不會出現重大錯誤陳述或損失。
and compliance with applicable laws and regulations, to identify
and manage potential risks, and to safeguard assets of the Group.
The risk management and internal control systems are designed to
manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business
objectives, and can only provide reasonable and not absolute
assurance against material misstatements or loss.

          Remuneration and benefits          We provide a highly competitive remuneration package in the industry, including
                                             basic salary, position salary, performance salary, five social insurances and
                                             one housing fund, commercial health insurance, accident insurance and other
                                             comprehensive benefits. Moreover, we also provide equity incentive schemes for
                                             employees with outstanding performance, allowing them to share the Company’s
                                             growth outcomes.
          薪酬及福利                              我們提供在業內極具競爭力的薪酬待遇，包括基本工資、崗位工資、績效工資、
                                             五險一金、商業健康及意外保險等全方位福利。此外，我們還為表現優異的員工
                                             提供股權激勵計劃，讓員工能夠分享公司的成長成果。

          Recruitment and promotion          We adhere to the principles of openness, fairness and impartiality and have
                                             established a comprehensive talent evaluation system. During the recruitment
                                             process, we comprehensively consider the applicants’ professional competence,
                                             work experience, personal qualities and development potential. In terms of
                                             promotion, we conduct comprehensive performance appraisals on a regular
                                             basis, providing outstanding talents with clear career development paths based
                                             on multiple dimensions such as their work performance, capabilities, teamwork
                                             and leadership.
          招聘及晉升                              我們堅持公開、公平、公正的原則，建立了完善的人才評估體系。在招聘過程
                                             中，我們綜合考量應聘者的專業能力、工作經驗、個人品格及發展潛力。在晉升
                                             方面，我們會定期進行全面的績效評估，根據員工的工作表現、能力、團隊協作
                                             及領導才能等多個維度，為優秀人才提供清晰的職業發展階梯。

          Working hours and rest periods     We strictly abide by the working hours stipulated by the Labor Law and have
                                             implemented a flexible working system so that employees can better balance
                                             work and life. In addition to statutory holidays, we provide additional paid annual
                                             leave, marriage leave, maternity leave/paternity leave, compassionate leave, etc.
                                             Meanwhile, we also support our employees to apply to work from home under
                                             special circumstances, reflecting our humane management.
          工作時數及假期                            我們嚴格遵守勞動法規定的工作時間，實行彈性工作制，讓員工能夠更好地平衡
                                             工作與生活。除法定節假日外，我們提供額外的帶薪年假、婚假、產假╱陪產
                                             假、恩恤假等。同時，我們也支持員工在特殊情況下申請在家辦公，體現人性化
                                             管理。

  Equal opportunities            We provide equal development opportunities for all employees, regardless of
                                 gender, age, race, religious beliefs, marital status, disability, etc. In every aspect
                                 such as promotion, training and remuneration adjustment, we insist on using
                                 ability and performance as the only criteria for judgment to ensure that every
                                 employee receives fair treatment and development opportunities.
  平等機會                           我們為所有員工提供平等的發展機會，不因性別、年齡、種族、宗教信仰、婚姻
                                 狀況、殘疾等因素而有所差異。在晉升、培訓、薪酬調整等各個環節，我們都
                                 堅持以能力和表現為唯一評判標準，確保每位員工都能獲得公平的對待和發展機
                                 會。

  Diversity policy               We firmly believe that a diverse team can bring more innovative thinking and
                                 competitive advantages. As such, we actively recruit talents from different
                                 cultural backgrounds, educational backgrounds and professional fields, and
                                 create an open and inclusive corporate culture through various team-building
                                 activities, cultural exchange programs, etc., so as to facilitate communication and
                                 cooperation among employees from different backgrounds.
  多元化政策                          我們深信多元化的團隊能夠帶來更多創新思維和競爭優勢。因此，我們積極聘用
                                 來自不同文化背景、教育經歷和專業領域的人才，並通過多樣化的團建活動、文
                                 化交流計劃等方式，營造開放包容的企業文化氛圍，促進不同背景員工之間的交
                                 流與合作。

  Anti-discrimination measures   We have zero tolerance towards any form of discrimination, harassment
                                 or inappropriate behavior in the workplace. The Company has a dedicated
                                 antidiscrimination policy and complaint mechanism to ensure that employees
                                 can work in a safe and respectful environment. We regularly conduct relevant
                                 training to enhance employees’ anti-discrimination awareness, and have set up a
                                 special working group to handle relevant complaints and suggestions in a timely
                                 manner. All complaints shall be dealt with promptly and fairly, while ensuring
                                 that the complainant’s privacy is fully protected.
  反歧視措施                          我們對工作場所的任何形式的歧視、騷擾或不當行為採取零容忍態度。公司設有
                                 專門的反歧視政策和申訴機制，確保員工能夠在安全、受尊重的環境中工作。我
                                 們定期開展相關培訓，提高員工的反歧視意識，並設立專門的工作小組，及時處
                                 理相關投訴和建議。所有投訴都將得到迅速、公正的處理，同時確保投訴人的隱
                                 私得到充分保護。

      Climate Change Management                                                氣候變化管理

      Climate change has become a major global concern, with potential         氣候變化已成為全球關注的重要議題，其潛在影響
      impacts including increased extreme weather events, energy               包括極端天氣事件增加、能源結構轉型以及氣候相
      structure transformation, and the continued strengthening of             關政策與監管要求的持續加強。上述因素可能對
      climate-related policies and regulations. These factors may              企業的營運環境、成本結構及市場需求帶來影響。
      affect a company’s operating environment, cost structure, and            為更好地識別及管理相關風險與機遇，本集團參考
      market demand. To better identify and manage related risks and           ISSB的建議框架，並結合國際研究機構及行業報
      opportunities, the Group, referencing the ISSB’s recommended             告，對氣候變化可能對本集團業務營運產生的影響
      framework and incorporating information from international               進行分析。
      research institutions and industry reports, has analyzed the potential
      impact of climate change on the Group’s business operations.

During the assessment process, the Group primarily categorized               在評估過程中，本集團將氣候相關風險主要劃分為
climate-related risks into two main types: physical risks and                實體風險及轉型風險兩大類。實體風險主要與極端
transition risks. Physical risks mainly relate to the impacts                氣候事件及長期氣候變化對基礎設施及營運環境造
of extreme weather events and long-term climate change on                    成的影響相關；轉型風險則主要與全球向低碳經濟
infrastructure and the operating environment; transitional risks             轉型過程中所帶來的政策、技術及市場變化有關。
mainly relate to policy, technological, and market changes brought
about by the global transition to a low-carbon economy.

Furthermore, with the rapid development of artificial intelligence and       此外，隨著人工智能及數字技術的快速發展，科技
digital technologies, technology companies also face new risk factors        企業在氣候變化方面亦面臨新的風險因素，例如算
related to climate change, such as increased energy consumption              力需求增加所帶來的能源消耗上升，以及數據中心
due to increased computing power demand and increased power                  電力需求增加等情況。本集團結合行業研究及自身
demand from data centers. Based on industry research and its own             業務特性，識別出以下與氣候變化相關的主要風險
business characteristics, the Group has identified the following key         及其潛在影響。
risks related to climate change and their potential impacts.

Given that the Group is still in the early stages of establishing          鑑於本集團目前仍處於建立氣候相關管理及數據收
climate-related management and data collection mechanisms,                 集機制的初期階段，相關分析所需的基礎數據、評
the basic data, assessment methods, and internal management                估方法及內部管理流程仍在持續完善之中。因此，
processes required for related analysis are still being continuously       本集團於本報告年度尚未開展全面的氣候情景分
improved. Therefore, the Group has not yet conducted a                     析，亦未對氣候相關風險及機遇可能帶來的財務影
comprehensive climate scenario analysis during the reporting               響進行量化評估。現階段，本集團主要透過年度風
year, nor has it quantified the potential financial impact brought         險識別及評估程序，初步識別可能影響本集團營運
by climate-related risks and opportunities. At this stage, the             的氣候相關實體風險及轉型風險，並將相關因素納
Group primarily uses its annual risk identification and assessment         入整體風險管理框架中進行持續監察。
procedures to initially identify climate-related physical risks
and transition risks that may affect the Group’s operations, and
incorporates these factors into the overall risk management
framework for continuous monitoring.

Furthermore, given that the Group’s business model primarily               此外，考慮到本集團的業務模式主要以數字內容服
focuses on digital content services and technology platform                務及技術平台營運為主，對高排放或高能源密集型
operations, and its reliance on high-emission or high-energy-              資產的依賴程度相對較低，因此目前氣候相關因素
intensive assets is relatively low, the direct impact of climate-related   對本集團財務表現的直接影響相對有限。然而，本
factors on the Group’s financial performance is currently relatively       集團仍將持續關注氣候政策、技術發展及市場轉型
limited. However, the Group will continue to monitor the potential         趨勢可能帶來的潛在影響。
impacts of climate policies, technological developments, and market
transformation trends.

Looking ahead, the Group will gradually improve its climate-related        展望未來，本集團將逐步完善氣候相關數據管理及
data management and analysis capabilities, and, where appropriate,         分析能力，並在適當情況下參考國際氣候相關披露
refer to international climate-related disclosure frameworks and           框架及監管機構建議，逐步開展氣候情景分析工
regulatory recommendations to gradually conduct climate scenario           作，並評估相關風險與機遇對本集團業務及財務表
analysis and assess the potential impact of related risks and              現的潛在影響，以持續提升氣候相關信息披露的完
opportunities on the Group’s business and financial performance, so        整性與透明度。
as to continuously enhance the completeness and transparency of
climate-related information disclosure.

       A1. Emissions
       A1.排放物
       General Disclosure   Information on:
                            (a)   the policies; and
                            (b)   compliance with relevant laws and regulations that have a significant
                                  impact on the issuer
                            relating to air and greenhouse gas emissions, discharges into water and land,
                            and generation of hazardous and non-hazardous waste.
       一般披露                 有關廢氣及溫室氣體排放、向水及土地的排污、有害及無害廢棄物的產生等的：
                            (a)   政策；及
                            (b)   遵守對發行人有重大影響的相關法律及規例的資料。
       A1.1                 The types of emissions and respective emissions data.
                            排放物種類及相關排放數據。
       A1.2                 Direct (Scope 1) and energy indirect (Scope 2) greenhouse gas emissions and
                            intensity.
                            直接（範圍1）及能源間接（範圍2）溫室氣體排放量及密度。
       A1.3                 Total hazardous waste produced and intensity.
                            所產生有害廢棄物總量及密度。
       A1.4                 Total non-hazardous waste produced and intensity.
                            所產生無害廢棄物總量及密度。
       A1.5                 Description of emissions target(s) set and steps taken to achieve them.
                            描述所訂立的排放量目標及為達到這些目標所採取的步驟。
       A1.6                 Description of how hazardous and non-hazardous wastes are handled, and a
                            description of reduction target(s) set and steps taken to achieve them.
                            描述處理有害及無害廢棄物的方法，及描述所訂立的減廢目標及為達到這些目
                            標所採取的步驟。

B7. Anti-corruption
B7.反貪污
General Disclosure    Information on:
                      (a)   the policies; and
                      (b)   compliance with relevant laws and regulations that have a significant
                            impact on the issuer
                      relating to bribery, extortion, fraud and money laundering.

                      於匯報期內對發行人或其僱員提出並已審結的貪污訴訟案件的數目及訴訟結果。

         Basis of consolidation                                                   綜合基準

         The consolidated financial statements include the                        綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司
         financial statements of the Company and its subsidiaries                 截至2025年12月31日止年度的財務報
         for the year ended 31 December 2025. A subsidiary is an                  表。附屬公司是指本公司直接或間接控
         entity (including a structured entity), directly or indirectly,          制的實體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團
         controlled by the Company. Control is achieved when the                  通過參與投資對象的相關活動而獲得或
         Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from                有權獲得可變回報，並且有能力運用對
         its involvement with the investee and has the ability to                 投資對象的權力（即賦予本集團現有能
         affect those returns through its power over the investee                 力以主導投資對象的相關活動的現有權
         (i.e., existing rights that give the Group the current ability           利）影響該等回報時，即取得控制權。
         to direct the relevant activities of the investee).

         Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of                     一般情況下均存在多數投票權形成控制
         voting rights results in control. When the Company has                   權的推定。倘本公司擁有少於投資對象
         less than a majority of the voting or similar rights of                  大多數投票或類似權利，則本集團於評
         an investee, the Group considers all relevant facts and                  估其是否擁有對投資對象的權力時會考
         circumstances in assessing whether it has power over an                  慮一切相關事實及情況，包括：
         investee, including:

         (a)   the contractual arrangement with the other vote                    (a)   與投資對象的其他投票權持有人
               holders of the investee;                                                 的合約安排；

         (b)   rights arising from other contractual                              (b)   其他合約安排所產生的權利；及
               arrangements; and

         (c)   the Group’s voting rights and potential voting                     (c)   本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。
               rights.

         The financial statements of the subsidiaries are                         附屬公司採用與本公司一致的會計政策
         prepared for the same reporting period as the Company,                   編製同一相關期間的財務報表。附屬公
         using consistent accounting policies. The results of                     司的業績由本集團獲得控制權之日起予
         subsidiaries are consolidated from the date on which the                 以綜合，並繼續綜合直至該控制權停止
         Group obtains control, and continue to be consolidated                   當日止。
         until the date that such control ceases.

               interests having a deficit balance. All intra-group assets          關的所有集團內公司間資產及負債、權

               control described above. A change in the ownership                  屬公司中不導致喪失控制權的所有者權

               any investment retained and any resulting surplus or                相應盈餘或虧絀。本集團應佔先前已在
               deficit in profit or loss. The Group’s share of components          其他全面收益確認的部分按與本集團直

               is reclassified to profit or loss or retained profits, as           重新分類至損益或保留溢利（如適用）。

         The amendments require disclosures of information                  率。該等修訂要求披露使財務報表使用

         Standards to report the effects of uncertainties in the            例反映相應香港財務報告準則會計準則
         financial statements using climate-related examples.               中關於使用氣候相關範例報告財務報表
         Therefore, the amendments do not have an effective date            不確定性影響的現有要求。因此，該等
         or transitional provisions.                                        修訂並無生效日期或過渡性條文。

           2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                      2.4 重要會計政策

               Investments in associates                                           於聯營公司之投資

               An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long             聯營公司為本集團長期擁有一般不少於
               term interest of generally not less than 20% of the equity          20%股本投票權，並對其有重大影響力
               voting rights and over which it has significant influence,          之實體。重大影響力乃有權參與投資對
               Significant influence is the power to participate in the            象之財務及經營政策決定而非控制或共
               financial and operating policy decisions of the investee,           同控制該等政策。
               but is not control or joint control over those policies.

               The Group’s investments in associates are stated in                 本集團於聯營公司的投資根據會計權益
               the consolidated statement of financial position at the             法按本集團分佔淨資產淨值減任何減值
               Group’s share of net assets under the equity method of              損失計算，並於綜合財務狀況表入賬。
               accounting, less any impairment losses.

               The Group’s share of the posit-acquisition results and              本集團應佔聯營公司之收購後業績及其
               other comprehensive income of associates is included                他全面收益分別計入綜合損益表及綜合
               in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and                 其他全面收益。此外，當於聯營公司之
               consolidated other comprehensive income, respectively.              股權直接確認變動時，本集團於綜合權
               In addition, when there has been a change recognised                益變動表確認其應佔之任何變動（倘適
               directly in the equity of the associate, the Group                  用）。本集團與其聯營公司進行之交易
               recognises its share of any changes, when applicable,               產生之未變現收益及損失，以本集團於
               in the consolidated statement of changes in equity.                 聯營公司之投資為限予以撇銷，惟倘未
               Unrealised gains and losses resulting from transactions             變現損失提供已轉讓資產之減值證據則
               between the Group and its associates are eliminated to              除外。收購聯營公司產生之商譽計入作
               the extent of the Group’s investments in the associates,            為本集團於聯營公司之部份投資。
               except where unrealised losses provide evidence of an
               impairment of the assets transferred. Goodwill arising
               from the acquisition of associates is included as part of
               the Group’s investments in associates.

               Upon loss of significant influence over the associate, the          若本集團失去對聯營公司的重大影響
               Group measures and recognises any retained investment               力，會按其公允價值計量及確認保留投
               at its fair value. Any difference between the carrying              資。於失去重大影響力時聯營公司的賬
               amount of the associate upon loss of significant influence          面值與保留投資公允價值及出售所得款
               and the fair value of the retained investment and                   項之間的差額於損益確認。
               proceeds from disposal is recognised in profit or loss.

               When an investment in an associate is classified as held            倘於聯營公司之投資分類為持作出售，
               for sale, it is accounted for in accordance with HKFRS 5            則根據香港財務報告準則第5號持作出
               Non-current Assets Held for Sale and Discontinued                   售及已終止業勨之非流動資產 入賬。

         Business combinations and goodwill                                  業務合併及商譽

         Business combinations are accounted for using the                   業務合併按收購法列賬。所轉讓代價乃
         acquisition method. The consideration transferred is                按收購日期的公平值計量，而該公平值
         measured at the acquisition date fair value which is                為本集團轉讓的資產於收購日期的公平
         the sum of the acquisition date fair values of assets               值、本集團自被收購方的前擁有人承擔
         transferred by the Group, liabilities assumed by the                的負債及本集團發行以換取被收購方控
         Group to the former owners of the acquiree and the                  制權的股本權益的總和。就各項業務合
         equity interests issued by the Group in exchange for                併而言，本集團選擇以公平值或被收購
         control of the acquiree. For each business combination,             方可識別資產淨值的應佔比例，計量於
         the Group elects whether to measure the non-                        被收購方的非控股權益。非控股權益的
         controlling interests in the acquiree at fair value or at           所有其他部分乃按公平值計量。收購相
         the proportionate share of the acquiree’s identifiable net          關成本於產生時支銷。
         assets. All other components of non-controlling interests
         are measured at fair value. Acquisition-related costs are
         expensed as incurred.

         The Group determines that it has acquired a business                當所收購的一組活動及資產包含共同對
         when the acquired set of activities and assets includes             創造產出的能力有重大貢獻的一項投入
         an input and a substantive process that together                    及一項實質性程序，本集團認為其已收
         significantly contribute to the ability to create outputs.          購一項業務。

         When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the                 本集團收購一項業務時，會根據於收購
         financial assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate            日期的合約條款、經濟狀況及相關條件
         classification and designation in accordance with                   評估所收購的金融資產及所承擔的金融
         the contractual terms, economic circumstances and                   負債，以作出適當分類及指定，包括區
         pertinent conditions as at the acquisition date. This               分被收購方主合同中的嵌入式衍生品。
         includes the separation of embedded derivatives in host
         contracts of the acquiree.

               Fair value measurement                                                公平值計量

               The Group measures its unlisted fund investment, wealth               本集團於各報告期末按公平值計量其非
               management product and unlisted equity investments at                 上市基金投資、理財產品及非上市股權
               fair value at the end of each reporting period. Fair value            投資。公平值指於計量日市場參與者之
               is the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid          間的有序交易中，就出售資產所收取或
               to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between             轉讓負債所支付的價格。公平值計量乃
               market participants at the measurement date. The fair                 基於假設出售資產或轉讓負債的交易於
               value measurement is based on the presumption that                    該資產或負債的主要市場或（在未有主
               the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability           要市場的情況下）該資產或負債的最有
               takes place either in the principal market for the asset              利市場進行。主要或最有利市場須為本
               or liability, or in the absence of a principal market, in             集團能參與的市場。假設市場參與者基
               the most advantageous market for the asset or liability.              於最佳經濟利益行事的情況下，資產或
               The principal or the most advantageous market must be                 負債的公平值乃使用市場參與者為資產
               accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a              或負債定價時所用的假設計量。
               liability is measured using the assumptions that market
               participants would use when pricing the asset or liability,
               assuming that market participants act in their economic
               best interest.

               A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes               非金融資產的公平值計量會考慮市場參
               into account a market participant’s ability to generate               與者將資產用於最高增值及最佳用途或
               economic benefits by using the asset in its highest and               將資產出售予會將資產用於最高增值及
               best use or by selling it to another market participant that          最佳用途的另一名市場參與者而創造經
               would use the asset in its highest and best use.                      濟利益的能力。

               The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate              本集團針對不同情況使用恰當的估值技
               in the circumstances and for which sufficient data are                術，確保有足夠數據可用於計量公平
               available to measure fair value, maximising the use of                值，並儘量利用相關可觀察輸入數據，
               relevant observable inputs and minimising the use of                  儘量減少使用不可觀察輸入數據。
               unobservable inputs.

         All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured          於財務報表計量或披露公平值的所有資
         or disclosed in the financial statements are categorised             產及負債，均基於對公平值計量整體而
         within the fair value hierarchy, described as follows,               言屬重大的最低層級輸入數據按下述公
         based on the lowest level input that is significant to the           平值層級分類：
         fair value measurement as a whole:

         Level 1 –    based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in                  第一層級－ 基於相同資產或負債於活
                      active markets for identical assets or                          躍市場的報價（未經調整）
                      liabilities


         Level 2 –    based on valuation techniques for which                 第二層級－ 基於就公平值計量而言屬
                      the lowest level input that is significant to                   重大的最低層級輸入數據
                      the fair value measurement is observable,                       可直接或間接觀察時的估
                      either directly or indirectly                                   值技術


         Level 3 –    based on valuation techniques for which the             第三層級－ 基於就公平值計量而言屬
                      lowest level input that is significant to the                   重大的最低層級輸入數據
                      fair value measurement is unobservable                          不可觀察時的估值技術

         For assets and liabilities that are recognised in the                就按經常性基準於財務報表確認的資產
         financial statements on a recurring basis, the Group                 及負債，本集團於各報告期末通過重新
         determines whether transfers have occurred between                   評估分類（基於對公平值計量整體而言
         levels in the hierarchy by reassessing categorisation                屬重大的最低層級輸入數據），確定有
         (based on the lowest level input that is significant to the          否在不同層級之間轉移。
         fair value measurement as a whole) at the end of each
         reporting period.

               amount is estimated. An asset’s recoverable amount is              產的可收回金額指資產或現金產生單位
               the higher of the asset’s or cash-generating unit’s value          的使用價值與其公平值減出售成本兩者
               in use and its fair value less costs of disposal, and is           之中的較高者，且按個別資產釐定，除
               determined for an individual asset, unless the asset does          非該資產所產生的現金流入並非基本上
               not generate cash inflows that are largely independent             獨立於其他資產或資產組別，在該情況
               of those from other assets or groups of assets, in which           下可收回金額則按資產所屬現金產生單
               case the recoverable amount is determined for the cash-            位釐定。
               generating unit to which the asset belongs.

               In testing a cash-generating unit for impairment, a                在對現金產生單位進行減值測試時，倘
               portion of the carrying amount of a corporate asset is             能按合理一致基準分配，則部分公司資
               allocated to an individual cash-generating unit if it can          產的賬面值分配至個別現金產生單位，
               be allocated on a reasonable and consistent basis or,              否則將分配至最小現金產生單位組別。
               otherwise, to the smallest group of cash-generating
               units.

               An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying              僅當資產的賬面值超逾其可收回金額時
               amount of an asset exceeds its recoverable amount. In              方會確認減值虧損。於評估使用價值
               assessing value in use, the estimated future cash flows            時，估計未來現金流量按可反映貨幣時
               are discounted to their present value using a pre-tax              間價值及資產特有風險的當前市場評估
               discount rate that reflects current market assessments             的稅前貼現率貼現至其現值。減值虧損
               of the time value of money and the risks specific to the           於產生期間在損益中列入與減值資產功
               asset. An impairment loss is charged to profit or loss in          能屬於同一類別的開支。
               the period in which it arises in those expense categories

               Impairment of financial assets                                          金融資產減值

               The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit                   本集團確認對並非按公平值計入損益計
               losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at                    量持有的所有債務工具預期信貸虧損
               fair value through profit or loss. ECLs are based on                    （「預期信貸虧損」）的撥備。預期信貸虧
               the difference between the contractual cash flows due                   損乃基於根據合約到期的合約現金流量
               in accordance with the contract and all the cash flows                  與本集團預期收取的所有現金流量之間
               that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an                     的差額而釐定，並以原實際利率的概約
               approximation of the original effective interest rate. The              值貼現。預期現金流量將包括出售所持
               expected cash flows will include cash flows from the sale               抵押的現金流量或組成合約條款的其他
               of collateral held or other credit enhancements that are                信貸提升措施。
               integral to the contractual terms.

               General approach                                                        一般方法

               ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures                 預期信貸虧損按兩個階段確認。就信貸
               for which there has not been a significant increase in                  風險自初步確認起並無顯著增加的信用
               credit risk since initial recognition, ECLs are provided                敞口而言，根據因未來12個月內可能
               for credit losses that result from default events that are              發生的違約事件導致的信貸虧損(12個
               possible within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL).                    月預期信貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損撥
               For those credit exposures for which there has been a                   備。就信貸風險自初步確認起已有顯著
               significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,          增加的有關信貸風險而言，則須就該敞
               a loss allowance is required for credit losses expected                 口的餘下年期預期信貸虧損計提虧損撥
               over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of the            備，而不論違約事件發生的時間（全期
               timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).                                 預期信貸虧損）。

         considers reasonable and supportable information that is              歷史和前瞻性資料。本集團認為，當合

         and forward-looking information. The Group considers                  大幅增加。
         that there has been a significant increase in credit risk
         when contractual payments are more than 30 days past
         due.

         taking into account any credit enhancements held by                   於無法合理預期可收回合約現金流量時
         the Group. A financial asset is written off when there is             撇銷。
         no reasonable expectation of recovering the contractual
         cash flows.

         Simplified approach                                                       簡化方法

         For trade receivables that do not contain a significant                   對於不包含重要融資組成部分的貿易應
         financing component or when the Group applies the                         收款項，或當本集團採取可行權宜方法
         practical expedient of not adjusting the effect of a                      不調整重要融資組成部分的影響時，本
         significant financing component, the Group applies the                    集團在計算預期信貸虧損時採用簡化方
         simplified approach in calculating ECLs. Under the                        法。根據簡化方法，本集團不跟蹤信貸
         simplified approach, the Group does not track changes                     風險的變化，而是在每個報告日期確認
         in credit risk, but instead recognises a loss allowance                   基於全期預期信貸虧損計算虧損撥備。
         based on lifetime ECLs at each reporting date. The Group                  本集團根據其歷史信貸虧損經驗建立了
         has established a provision matrix that is based on its                   一個撥備矩陣，並根據債務人具體的前
         historical credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-                  瞻性因素和經濟環境加以調整。
         looking factors specific to the debtors and the economic
         environment.

         Financial liabilities                                                     金融負債

         Initial recognition and measurement                                       初步確認及計量

         Financial liabilities are classified, at initial recognition, as          金融負債初步確認時分類為貸款及借款
         loans and borrowings, or payables, as appropriate.                        或應付款項（如適用）。

         All financial liabilities are recognised initially at fair value          所有金融負債初步按公平值確認，貸款
         and, in the case of loans and borrowings and payables,                    及借款以及應付款項則須扣除直接應佔
         net of directly attributable transaction costs.                           交易成本。

         The Group’s financial liabilities include trade and other                 本集團的金融負債包括貿易及其他應付
         payables, lease liabilities and interest-bearing bank and                 款項、租賃負債以及計息銀行及其他借
         other borrowings.                                                         款。

               cost, using the effective interest rate method unless the              貼現影響不重大，在該情況下，則按成
               effect of discounting would be immaterial, in which case               本列賬。收益及虧損於負債終止確認時
               they are stated at cost. Gains and losses are recognised               並通過實際利率攤銷程序於損益確認。
               in profit or loss when the liabilities are derecognised as
               well as through the effective interest rate amortisation
               process.

               Amortised cost is calculated by taking into account any                計算攤銷成本時，考慮收購產生的任何
               discount or premium on acquisition and fees or costs                   折讓或溢價以及作為實際利率組成部分
               that are an integral part of the effective interest rate. The          的費用或成本。實際利率攤銷則計入損
               effective interest rate amortisation is included in finance            益的融資成本。
               costs in profit or loss.

               Derecognition of financial liabilities                                 終止確認金融負債

               A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation              金融負債於負債責任解除、取消或屆滿
               under the liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.            時終止確認。

               When an existing financial liability is replaced by another            倘現有金融負債被另一項由同一借貸人
               from the same lender on substantially different terms,                 提供但絕大部分條款不同的負債所取
               or the terms of an existing liability are substantially                代，或現有負債的條款經大幅修訂，則
               modified, such an exchange or modification is treated as               有關取代或修訂以終止確認原有負債並
               a derecognition of the original liability and a recognition            確認新負債的方式處理，而相關賬面值
               of a new liability, and the difference between the                     的差額於損益確認。
               respective carrying amounts is recognised in profit or
               loss.

3.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES                                  3.   重大會計估計

     The preparation of the Group’s financial statements requires           編製本集團財務報表時，管理層須作出影響
     management to make judgements, estimates and assumptions               所呈報收益、開支、資產及負債金額及其
     that affect the reported amounts of revenues, expenses, assets         隨附披露及或然負債披露的判斷、估計及假

19. TRADE RECEIVABLES (Continued)                                         19. 貿易應收款項（續）

    An impairment analysis is performed at each reporting date                   於各報告日期使用撥備矩陣進行減值分析，
    using a provision matrix to measure expected credit losses.                  以計量預期信貸虧損。撥備率基於具有類似
    The provision rates are based on days past due for groupings of              虧損模式的不同客戶分部分組的預期天數（即
    various customer segments with similar loss patterns (i.e., by               按地理區域、產品類型、客戶類型及評級）。
    geographical region, product type, customer type and rating).                該計算反映概率加權結果、貨幣的時間價值
    The calculation reflects the probability-weighted outcome,                   及於報告日期可獲得有關過往事件、當前狀
    the time value of money and reasonable and supportable                       況及未來經濟狀況預測的合理有據的資料。
    information that is available at the reporting date about past               一般而言，倘貿易應收款項逾期超過一年，
    events, current conditions and forecasts of future economic                  則會予以撇銷，且不受強制執行活動所規限。
    conditions. Generally, trade receivables are written off if past
    due for more than one year and are not subject to enforcement
    activity.

    Set out below is the information about the credit risk exposure              下表載列本集團採用撥備矩陣計量貿易應收

    term maturities of these instruments.

    instruments. The corporate finance team reports directly to            期，公司財務部對金融工具的價值變動進行
    the chief financial officer. At each reporting date, the corporate     分析，並決定估值採用的主要輸入數據。估

    instruments and determines the major inputs applied in the

      36. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES                               36. 金融風險管理目標及政策
          AND POLICIES

          The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise cash            本集團的主要金融工具包括現金及現金等價
          and cash equivalents, and interest-bearing bank and other            物及計息銀行及其他借款。該等金融工具的
          borrowings. The main purpose of these financial instruments          主要目的是為本集團的運營籌集資金。本集
          is to raise finance for the Group’s operations. The Group has        團擁有多項其他金融資產及負債，例如直接
          various other financial assets and liabilities such as trade         自其運營產生的貿易應收款項、計入預付款
          receivables, financial assets included in prepayments, deposits      項、按金及其他應收款項的金融資產、貿易
          and other receivables, trade payables and financial liabilities      應付款項及計入其他應付款項及應計費用的
          included in other payables and accruals, which arise directly        金融負債。
          from its operations.

          The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments        本集團的金融工具所涉及的主要風險為信貸
          are credit risk and liquidity risk. The directors review and         風險及流動資金風險。董事檢討並同意管理
          agree policies for managing each of these risks and they are         各項此等風險的政策，該等政策的概要如下。
          summarised below.

          Credit risk                                                          信貸風險

          The Group trades only with recognised and creditworthy third         本集團僅與經認可且信譽良好的第三方進行
          parties. It is the Group’s policy that all customers who wish        交易。本集團的政策規定，所有擬按信貸期
          to trade on credit terms are subject to credit verification          進行交易的客戶，均須接受信貸核實程序。
          procedures. In addition, receivable balances are monitored on        此外，本集團持續監察應收款項結餘，面對
          an ongoing basis and the Group’s exposure to bad debts is not        的壞賬風險並不重大。
          significant.

          Maximum exposure and year-end staging                                最大風險及年末的分級

          exposure to credit risk based on the Group’s credit policy,          及就信貸風險所承擔的最大風險，主要以已
          which is mainly based on past due information unless other           逾期的資料為依據（除非有其他資料而毋須使
          information is available without undue cost or effort, and year-     用過多成本或努力），以及於12月31日的年
          end staging classification as at 31 December. The amounts            末分級分類。已呈報金額為金融資產的賬面
